# All checkpoint selections collapse to Huber on a perturbed dyadic family

Status: COMPLETE JOINT UNVERIFIED analytical draft, max2 round51.
Assignment: TASK.md and master_guidance4c8674478c3e4a53.
Plan:20537eac824926ce. No fact_submit is authorized or made.

The new claim is about the definite scalar functional in frozen formula(5),
including its entire parameter domain and every eligible checkpoint subset.
It is NOT an upper bound for actual gradient-descent error W. It says nothing
about the stronger true-radius formula(4), the separate source-format
formula(5a), or the sufficiency of the actual spiral functions. In particular
it does not resolve the original minimax problem.

All new lemmas below are proved together in this one draft; none is treated
as an independently accepted fact. The only accepted mathematical input is
fact04219554e41d9750, for the unchanged benchmark ray and its certified
enclosures. The spiral geometry and its sibling audit are not premises.

## 1. Complete scalar definition and result

For 0<kappa<=epsilon<=1/2 set

    Theta=arctan(1/epsilon),
    C=c_sp=sqrt(1+epsilon^2)*exp(kappa*Theta),
    d_sp=1-kappa^2,
    T_sp=(exp(d_sp*Theta/kappa)-1)/d_sp,
    H_sp=C*(1+(1+kappa)*T_sp)-1,
    A=A_sp=epsilon+(H_sp+1)/epsilon.

Here C and A are local abbreviations for the source's explicit constants;
C is not the benchmark sequence C_n. The source is frozen finding
cdd7f057606aeb18 at
/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/cdd7f057606aeb18.md,
SHA256366cd6a12c8e4f56cf3c875c104f3e9bfdc185bf26c955e4f7e83d5bef7b607c.
That hash was read back and matched. No extension of the parameter range
or change in the constant formula is made here.

For a finite nonnegative physical word h, a selected subset is written in
its actual chronological order as

    h=(block_0,b_0,block_1,b_1,...,block_(k-1),b_(k-1),block_k).

The ordinary sums s_i are sums of exactly those intervening unselected
steps. Each selected checkpoint must satisfy b_i>=1+epsilon^(-2).
Define Q(b)=max{epsilon*b,A} and

    Phi(h;epsilon,kappa,selection)
      =1/(1+2*s_k)*product_(i=0)^(k-1)
          [(b_i-1)/(C*s_i+Q(b_i))]^2.                 (1)

Empty blocks are allowed. For k=0 this is exactly1/(1+2*sum h).
Let L_sp(h) be the supremum of(1) over all these subsets and all
0<kappa<=epsilon<=1/2, including k=0. No geometry is required to define it.

Put lambda=1+sqrt(2). For a fixed real tau>0 and an integer m>tau set

    beta=1-tau/m, c=lambda*(1+tau/m), a=beta/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m.       (2)

Every child in(2) keeps the SAME enclosing(m,tau). Write H_(m,tau)=H_m,
n_m=2^m-1, and S_j=sum H_j. These are exactly the words specified in
TASK.md. Only tau=1 is the original diagnostic word; the values used below
are new explicitly specified words in the permitted family.

CLAIM. For EVERY real0<tau<=1/4 and EVERY integer m>=1536,

    L_sp(H_(m,tau))=1/(1+2*S_m).                      (3)

More strongly, at EACH individual allowed parameter pair, every eligible
selection is bounded above by the right side of(3). Thus the full parameter
supremum, including parameters depending arbitrarily on the whole word and
horizon, is controlled by a finite statement, not an interchange of a limit
and a supremum.

Retain the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark

    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4*x*y))/2,
    G(x,y)=2*x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8*x*y)/2,
    C_0=B_0=1,
    C_n=max_(0<=j<n) Omega(C_j,C_(n-j-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=j<n) G(C_j,B_(n-j-1)).

All split indices are integers, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. Accepted
fact04219554e41d9750 establishes the positive finite ray limit

    B_(2^m-1)/lambda^m -> Z_1.

For every fixed0<tau<=1/4, the resulting exact comparison is

    lim_(m->infinity) B_(n_m)*L_sp(H_(m,tau))
                   =Z_1/(2*exp(tau)).               (4)

In particular the single explicit choice tau=1/4 gives

    lim B_(n_m)*L_sp(H_(m,1/4))<237/250<1.            (5)

Every fixed tau in[1/5,1/4] also gives a limit below79/80. These strict
constants use only the already certified benchmark enclosure, restated
and checked from its saved endpoints in Section7. No new benchmark value,
dynamic program, numerical experiment, parameter scan, solver or
mathematical program is evaluated.

## 2. Exact sums and the complete children next to a selected minimum

For the fixed enclosing(m,tau), let

    v=a/(c-2)=beta/[c(c-2)],
    M_j=1+S_j,
    d_j=a*c^j,
    b_j=1+d_j,
    u_j=d_j/M_j,
    M_max=M_(m-1), r=c(c-2).

The subscript j here is TREE LEVEL, not the physical time of a particular
occurrence. Every occurrence of b_j has an immediate complete H_j child
on each side. The physical length of H_j is2^j-1.

Direct induction in(2), starting from M_0=1, gives

    M_j=v*c^j+(1-v)*2^j.                             (6)

Indeed the sum recurrence is S_(j+1)=2*S_j+1+a*c^j, and therefore
M_(j+1)=2*M_j+a*c^j. Substituting(6) and using a=v(c-2) proves the next
case. This is a symbolic identity, not an executed recurrence.

With x=tau/m>0 and gamma=4+2*sqrt(2),

    r=1+gamma*x+lambda^2*x^2>1>beta>0.

Thus0<v<1 and c>lambda>2. Formula(6) yields

    1<=M_j<=c^j,
    d_j/M_j<=a/v=c-2,
    M_j<=M_max for0<=j<m.                            (7)

The last inequality also follows directly from the positive sum recurrence.

Now take ANY nonempty selected subset and choose one of its checkpoints
with globally minimum tree level j. Neither adjacent H_j child contains
a selected checkpoint, since every step in those children has level<j.
Consequently the ordinary sums s and t immediately before and after this
chosen checkpoint satisfy

    s>=S_j, t>=S_j.                                 (8)

If it is the first selected checkpoint, s means the actual initial block;
if it is the last, t means the actual terminal block. Both still contain
the complete adjacent child. Tied minimum levels cause no difficulty:
their complete children contain strictly smaller levels. This observation
uses no assumption about records, greedy selection, independence, or an
optimal split of a different recurrence.

## 3. An exact deletion lemma, retaining the next checkpoint cost

Here suppose2<c<=5/2, and suppose C>=1,A>=C are fixed for the whole word.
Keep Q(b)=max{epsilon*b,A}; for this lemma epsilon may be any nonnegative
number. The proof does not change any remaining checkpoint or its parameter.

For a selected minimum-level checkpoint b=b_j=1+d, put

    M=M_j, S=M-1, u=d/M.

By(7),0<u<=c-2<=1/2. The costs Q(b), and Q of any next checkpoint,
are at least A.

First suppose there IS a next selected checkpoint b', with R=Q(b').
All earlier and later factors in(1) cancel when b is deleted. The exact
local comparison of the positive, unsquared factors is

       d/(C*s+Q(b)) * (b'-1)/(C*t+R)
    <= (b'-1)/(C*(s+b+t)+R)                          (9)

if and only if

    d*[C*(s+b+t)+R] <= (C*s+Q(b))*(C*t+R).           (10)

This pays for the entire deleted physical step b, including its added1.
It also keeps the ACTUAL next checkpoint cost R, even if that step is huge.

To prove(10), subtract its left side from its right side. The difference
is increasing in Q(b). Its derivative with respect to R is C*s+Q(b)-d.
Its derivatives with respect to s and t are respectively
C*(C*t+R-d) and C*(C*s+Q(b)-d). All are positive on the required region:
by(8), C*s+Q(b)>=C*(M-1)+A>=C*M>=M>=2*d, and the same holds for C*t+R.
Therefore the smallest difference occurs at

    s=t=S, Q(b)=R=A.

Define z=(A-C)/M>=0. At these values the difference, divided by M^2, is

    E(C,u,z)=(C+z)^2-u*[C*(2+u)+z]
            =C^2-C*u*(2+u)+z*(2*C-u)+z^2.            (11)

This proves that either of the following WHOLE-WORD conditions suffices
for deleting every selected minimum-level interior checkpoint:

    (I)  C>=c(c-2);
    (II) A>=2*C*M_max.                               (12)

For(I), u*(2+u)<=c(c-2)<=C; the first two terms of(11) have nonnegative
sum, and the other terms are nonnegative because2*C-u>0.

For(II), M<=M_max and M_max>=1 imply

    z=(A-C)/M>=C*(2*M_max-1)/M>=C.

Using u<=1/2, C>=1, z>=C, and2*C-u>0 in(11) gives

    E(C,u,z)>=C^2-(5/4)*C+C*(2*C-1/2)+C^2
              =4*C^2-(7/4)*C>0.                     (13)

Thus(9) holds in either case. Squaring preserves the comparison, all
unchanged factors are nonnegative, and deleting b cannot lower Phi.

## 4. The last selected checkpoint has a different exact comparison

If the minimum-level checkpoint b is LAST, deletion changes the terminal
Huber denominator. Its exact comparison is

       d^2/[(C*s+Q(b))^2*(1+2*t)]
    <= 1/[1+2*(s+b+t)].                             (14)

Since C>=1 and A>=C, C*s+Q(b)>=s+1. It suffices to bound

    d^2*[1+2*(s+b+t)]/[(s+1)^2*(1+2*t)]<=1.          (15)

For fixed s>=0, the fraction without d^2 equals

    [1+2*(s+b)/(1+2*t)]/(s+1)^2,

so it is decreasing in t. Its derivative with respect to s, with the
positive constant1+2*t suppressed, is

    -2*(s+2*b+2*t)/(s+1)^3<0.

It is therefore largest at s=t=S=M-1, by(8). At that endpoint(15) has
left side

    u^2*[(4+2*u)*M-1]/(2*M-1)
      <=u^2*(3+2*u)<=1,                             (16)

because M>=1 and0<u<=1/2. For the first inequality the difference after
multiplying by2*M-1 is2*(1+u)*(M-1)>=0. For the second, u^2*(3+2*u)
is increasing on[0,1/2] and equals1 at1/2.

Unlike the interior case, this needs neither condition(I) nor(II).
It handles the sole selected checkpoint and the final stage of pruning.

Under either condition in(12), repeatedly choose a currently selected
minimum level and apply Section3 or Section4. The number of selections
strictly decreases, so after finitely many TEXTUAL deletion steps the
selection is empty and its payoff has not decreased. This is an argument
for an arbitrary finite subset, not an executed enumeration or algorithm.
In particular, for EACH fixed parameter pair satisfying(12),

    Phi<=1/(1+2*S_m).                               (17)

No eligibility is lost by deletion: all remaining checkpoints and both
parameters stay unchanged. Threshold equalities are retained. At level0
the children are empty, S_0=0 and M_0=1; all denominators and inequalities
above remain defined, with no division by S_j. The target words have no
zero physical steps, and no argument treats a zero step as positive.

## 5. The frozen spiral constants force the deletion dichotomy uniformly

We now prove(12) for EVERY allowed epsilon,kappa whenever
0<tau<=1/4 and m>=1536.

First, c<5/2. Indeed sqrt(2)<10/7, so lambda<17/7, and for m>=9,

    c<=lambda*(1+1/(4*m))
      <(17/7)*(37/36)=629/252<5/2.                  (18)

Also gamma<7, lambda^2<6, and tau<=1/4 give, for m>=2,

    r-1=gamma*tau/m+lambda^2*tau^2/m^2
      <7/(4*m)+3/(8*m^2)
      <=31/(16*m)<2/m.                             (19)

The explicit source formula simplifies exactly. Put X=d_sp*Theta/kappa.
Since d_sp=(1-kappa)*(1+kappa),

    1+(1+kappa)*T_sp=(exp(X)-kappa)/(1-kappa),
    A=epsilon+(C/epsilon)*(exp(X)-kappa)/(1-kappa)
       >=(C/epsilon)*exp(X).                        (20)

The last inequality follows from
(exp(X)-kappa)/(1-kappa)-exp(X)
=kappa*(exp(X)-1)/(1-kappa)>=0. In particular C>1 and A>=C/epsilon>=2*C,
so the baseline assumptions of Sections3--4 hold.

If C>=r, condition(I) already holds. It remains to consider EVERY
parameter pair with C<r, without choosing or optimizing one scaling.
Since C>=sqrt(1+epsilon^2), equations(19) and m>=2 give

    epsilon^2<=C^2-1<r^2-1<4/m+4/m^2<=6/m.

For m>=1536 this implies epsilon<1/16, hence kappa<=epsilon<1/16 and

    d_sp>255/256,
    Theta=pi/2-arctan(epsilon)>3/2-epsilon>23/16.    (21)

Here pi>3 follows, for example, from the strictly shorter inscribed
regular hexagon, and arctan(epsilon)<epsilon follows by integrating
1/(1+t^2)<1 for t>0. These are strict elementary inequalities.

The SAME assumption C<r gives

    kappa*Theta<=log C<log r<r-1<2/m.

Consequently

    X=d_sp*Theta/kappa
       >(d_sp*Theta^2/2)*m
       >(134895/131072)*m>m.                        (22)

The displayed rational is255*529/(256*256*2); its numerator exceeds
its denominator. Combining(20)--(22) gives

    A>16*C*exp(m).                                  (23)

Finally exp(1)>1+1+1/2=5/2>c, while M_max<=c^(m-1) by(7). Thus

    A>16*C*exp(m)>16*C*c^(m-1)>=2*C*M_max.           (24)

This is condition(II). Equations(18)--(24) prove the dichotomy(12)
simultaneously for all allowed parameters. In particular they include
kappa*m tending to zero, tending to infinity, bounded nonconvergent
scalings, fixed parameters, equality kappa=epsilon, and epsilon=1/2.
The proof makes no asymptotic assumption on the parameter choices.

For each pair, (17) bounds every selection, while the empty selection
attains its right side. Taking both suprema proves the exact identity(3).

## 6. Exact physical horizon and asymptotic comparison

For a fixed tau in(0,1/4], equation(6) at depth m gives

    1+2*S_m=2*v*c^m+2*(1-v)*2^m-1.

As m tends to infinity, beta tends1, r tends1, and v=beta/r tends1.
Furthermore c^m/lambda^m=(1+tau/m)^m tends exp(tau). The second term
after division by lambda^m tends0 because0<1-v<1 and2/lambda<1;
lambda^(-m) also tends0. Therefore

    (1+2*S_m)/lambda^m ->2*exp(tau).                 (25)

The accepted ray limit from fact04219554e41d9750 is for exactly the
physical length n_m=2^m-1, with no hidden offset, rescaling, or different
benchmark recurrence. Combining it with(3) and(25) proves(4).

Using Z_1<237/100 as justified in Section7 and exp(1/4)>1+1/4=5/4 gives

    Z_1/(2*exp(1/4))< (237/100)/(5/2)=237/250<1.

For a fixed tau in[1/5,1/4], exp(tau)>=exp(1/5)>6/5 gives the parallel
bound Z_1/(2*exp(tau))<237/240=79/80.

Thus this draft supplies a full-supremum obstruction for frozen MASS
formula(5). It gives no upper bound on W_(n_m)(H_(m,tau)). Weakness of a
lower-bound formula is not a small-error certificate for its word.

## 7. Only saved, accepted benchmark data are used

Section8 of accepted fact04219554e41d9750 certifies the public table
/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/audit_c78/input_interval_table.csv,
SHA2562a7585bc0bade9f85461456c13d60bbe618f85c7cc816e1ddc5b4c1a1d611e68.
The saved rows1023 and1024 and the hash were read, with no replay of its
generator, exact checker, dynamic program, or any benchmark calculation.

For D80=1208925819614629174706176 its saved upper endpoint is

    D80*B_1024<=H1024=19229705732238461978760749948
                    <15910*D80.                    (26)

For transparency the last product is the exact integer

    15910*D80=19234009790068750169575260160,

whose difference from H1024 is positive. This is a written comparison of
an already certified endpoint, not a new benchmark evaluation.

The square comparison19600<19602 proves sqrt(2)>140/99. Repeated
multiplication, or the identity lambda^2=2*lambda+1, gives

    lambda^10=3363+2378*sqrt(2)>6725.                (27)

Indeed (3363*99+2378*140)-6725*99=82>0.

The accepted enclosure, applied at b=1,j=10, is

    Z_1<=lambda^(-10)*(sqrt(B_1024)+3/100)^2.

Equations(26)--(27), sqrt(15910)<127, and the exact rational comparisons

    15910+381/50+9/10000<15918,
    15918*100=1591800<1593825=6725*237

imply

    Z_1<(sqrt(15910)+3/100)^2/6725
         <15918/6725<237/100.                        (28)

This is the same already supplied Z_1 enclosure used in sibling finding
8880a32b8502cf2f, with its short derivation repeated here from the accepted
fact and certified table, so that sibling draft is not a premise.

## 8. Optional exact path representation and boundary conventions

For an arbitrary nonnegative word h of length n and fixed allowed
parameters, let E_h be the set of eligible physical indices. Adjoin a
virtual source index-1 and put V_(-1)=1. For q in E_h in chronological
order define

    S(p,q)=sum_(p<t<q) h_t,
    V_q=(h_q-1)^2 * max_(p in{-1} union E_h, p<q)
          V_p/[C*S(p,q)+Q(h_q)]^2.                  (29)

Then the exact best value at these fixed parameters is

    max_(p in{-1} union E_h)
        V_p/[1+2*sum_(t>p)h_t].                     (30)

For the virtual source, S(-1,q) is the entire prefix before q, and the
tail in(30) is the entire word. The proof of(29) is to condition on the
penultimate selected checkpoint; the last factor is then exactly the
displayed edge weight. Induction on q accounts for every chronological
subset. Conditioning on the final selected checkpoint proves(30).
When n=0, the sole term is1. When an intervening sum is0, its denominator
is Q(h_q)>=A>0; zero physical steps simply occur in these ordinary sums.
Eligibility is the non-strict inequality, so threshold equalities are
included. No child parameter is changed.

This recurrence was NOT executed. It is not the comparison result:
Sections2--5 prove the stronger path domination needed for(3), by a
directionally justified deletion on the actual physical word.

## 9. Stress tests, scope, and handoff

The deletion proof was checked symbolically at the boundary cases that
could invalidate it: a first selected checkpoint, a sole selected
checkpoint, a last selected checkpoint, tied minimum levels, level0 and
empty children, and arbitrarily large Q of the next selected checkpoint.
The distinct terminal calculation(14)--(16) cannot be replaced by the
interior calculation. All comparisons keep the deleted step's full
physical size1+d. No assertion about the optimality of a record chain
or of a greedy policy occurs.

The result is deliberately for0<tau<=1/4, and does not establish(3) at
tau=1. It does not transfer any older ray/checkpoint obstruction to this
new denominator. It does not claim a consequence for formula(4),
formula(5a), or for any enlarged geometric parameter domain.

Retrieval found a generic max-plus path expansion in Proposition4 of
arXiv:2102.12264 and unrelated results for the deletion query. None
supplies or is used for this proof. The proof instead stays inside the
already specified scalar functional, finite hierarchy, and elementary
inequalities; no external theorem or unverified geometric construction
is a building block. The project glossary search returned no matches
for the new spiral/deletion notation, so all local symbols are defined
here. This is a jointly derived UNVERIFIED finding, not a verifier verdict.

The assigned one analytical round ends with(3)--(5). All mathematical
computation and formal gates remain closed. No installation, renewal,
finalization, publication, fact-store edit, or swarm control is performed.

## Glossary

L_sp: supremum of the frozen mass functional(1), over all eligible subsets
and exactly0<kappa<=epsilon<=1/2, including the empty subset.
Phi: the scalar selection payoff(1).
Theta,c_sp,d_sp,T_sp,H_sp,A_sp: exactly the frozen constants in Section1.
C,A: local abbreviations c_sp,A_sp, not benchmark C_n or seed capacity.
Q(b): max{epsilon*b,A}, the exact target-checkpoint cost in(1).
lambda:1+sqrt(2).
tau,m,beta,c,a,H_j,H_(m,tau),n_m: physical word parameters and hierarchy(2).
S_j,M_j: sum of the fixed enclosing word H_j, and1+S_j.
v: a/(c-2)=beta/[c(c-2)].
d_j,b_j,u_j: level-j checkpoint excess a*c^j, its physical step1+d_j,
and d_j/M_j.
M_max: M_(m-1), largest child mass in the depth-m word.
r: c(c-2).
gamma:4+2*sqrt(2), the coefficient in r=1+gamma*tau/m+lambda^2*tau^2/m^2.
s,t: actual ordinary sums immediately before and after a removed checkpoint.
b,d,M,S,u,z,R,E(C,u,z): local deletion notation in Sections3--4; R is the
next checkpoint's Q cost and E is the exact normalized difference(11).
X: d_sp*Theta/kappa, the activation exponent in Section5.
Omega,G,C_n,B_n,U_n^ZJ,Z_1: unchanged benchmark quantities from accepted
fact04219554e41d9750, fully restated in Section1.
D80,H1024: denominator and saved certified upper endpoint in Section7.
E_h,V_p,S(p,q): eligible indices, maximal partial product, and exact
ordinary gap sum in the unexecuted path representation(29)--(30).
W_n: original worst-case normalized GD error, mentioned only to delimit
scope; no upper estimate on it is asserted.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000428 -->
# max67: an asymmetric exact-copy transport and its full-objective obstruction

Status: complete joint analytical draft, UNVERIFIED. There is no new fact,
formal submission, unrestricted objective upper bound, or actual lower
witness. The original minimax problem remains OPEN.

This round tests one specific asymmetric transport. It copies the right
child's finite interpolation coefficients, scales its optimum attachments
separately, and permits arbitrary position-dependent corrections throughout
the left block. Its sole repair adds forward cross-block mass and makes the
backward transport affine in the right child's chronological position.

The outcome is a full-original-space PSD obstruction, not just a failed
bridge inequality. For every actual enclosing m>=1536, every sign-valid,
value-cancelling completion in the specified family would require

                  q_cert*(1+2 sum H_m) > 11/10.                 (0)

The same conclusion covers the unrepaired rule. With the original benchmark,
any sequence of PSD levels within this family would satisfy

       liminf_m B_(2^m-1)*q_cert,m > 3619/3600 > 1.             (1)

Thus this particular rule cannot supply the required improvement at
unbounded true horizons. It does not exclude different terminal destinations,
a different right optimum scale, corrections to the right finite block,
non-affine transport, optimum-outgoing coefficients at a larger objective
level, or general asymmetric full certificates.

## 1. Exact physical words, verified inputs, and the viability requirement

Fix one enclosing integer m. Throughout the entire hierarchy use

 lambda_s=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4m),
 c=lambda_s*(1+1/(4m)), a=beta_word/c,
 H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m.

Every occurrence of a child uses these SAME parameters. The number of
physical updates in H_m is n_m=2^m-1. The terminal gradient is a formal
interpolation variable; it supplies no extra update.

The accepted mathematical inputs are fact1252b8823bb5a290, for the original
objective cone; fact04219554e41d9750, for the unconditional original benchmark
and its certified interval table; and fact5fd396eba75651e7, for the stated
Huber/benchmark comparison on these exact words. No conditional seed clause
of fact042 is used. The two numerical/analytical findings242f8d453ac26183
and dd4045b39aa0c257 are provenance and awareness, not correctness premises.

Before attempting a recursive PSD proof, a prospective whole-word level
q_cert=chi/(1+2 sum H_m) must be checked at the original B_n scale. The
accepted inequality B_(n_m)/(1+2 sum H_m)<237/250 would suffice with a
uniform chi<250/237. Multiplying a fixed loss greater than one at every
depth does not meet this condition. For example (101/100)^m>=1+m/100,
so an assertion of a one-percent loss at each level cannot be replaced
by an assertion of a one-percent whole-word loss. The obstruction below
is stronger: even the loss needed at the final join is too large at the
actual limiting B_n scale.

All new calculations in this document are symbolic textual derivations.
No schedule, recurrence, matrix, spectrum, fitted coefficient, or benchmark
has been evaluated by a mathematical program in this round.

## 2. Original cone and an exact full matrix formula

Normalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For independent formal vectors
(x_0,g_0,...,g_n), define x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. All distinct ordered
original interpolation rows remain in the model:

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

Let lambda_ij be their nonnegative multipliers. Values cancel in

 M(q)=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij*Q_ij               (2)

exactly when out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n) at every finite i. A valid upper
certificate requires PSD on the ENTIRE original formal space, as specified
in fact1252. In the tested family lambda_(star,i)=0. Write
nu_i=lambda_(i,star), and z_i=-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. Once flow cancels values,
the complete quadratic, with every original gradient retained, is

 M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i nu_i<g_i,x_0>
      +sum_i nu_i[-<g_i,z_i>+||g_i||^2/2]
      +sum_(i!=j finite)lambda_ij
           [<g_i,z_j-z_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2].                 (3)

Thus its full matrix is [[q,-nu^T/2],[-nu/2,C]], where C acts on
all n+1 finite gradient coordinates. If h_n=0, its entries are

 C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i*sum_(j>i)lambda_ij,
 2C_ij=-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji)-h_j*sum_(v>j)lambda_iv
          +h_i*(nu_j+sum_(v<=i)lambda_jv), i<j.             (4)

The sums in (4) are over finite labels; out_i includes nu_i. To verify
the diagonal, the squared gradient differences contribute half the incident
mass, which is out_i-1_(i=n) by flow. A forward position term based at i
contributes -h_i*lambda_ij. For i<j, squared differences contribute
-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji) to the cross coefficient. Position terms based at i
with destination after j contribute -h_j times their multipliers; those
based at j with destination at or before i, including the optimum,
contribute h_i times their multipliers. This proves (3)-(4) directly.

Zeros assigned below are restrictions on this candidate's coefficients,
not assertions that any original interpolation row is redundant.

## 3. One asymmetric transport; exactly one affine repair

All quantities introduced in this section are local to the displayed join;
in particular its gradient matrix C is distinct from the benchmark C_n.
Consider a parent (H_j,d,H_j), where k=2^j-1, j>=1, and d=1+a*c^j.
Let the child steps be u_0,...,u_(k-1), with

 p_i=sum_(v<i)u_v, 0<=i<=k, A=p_k>0,
 t=A+d, S=2A+d, D=1+2S, D_child=1+2A.

Left finite labels are L_l=l, 0<=l<=k; L_k is the physical bridge
query. Right finite labels are R_i=k+1+i, 0<=i<=k. The actual parent
terminal is R_k=2k+1. Their chronological masses are p_l and t+p_i.

Take ANY nonnegative reference finite array K_ij (i!=j, 0<=i,j<=k), K_ii=0,
and nonnegative optimum attachment vector mu_i satisfying

 sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+mu_i=2*1_(i=k).                    (5)

Summing (5) gives sum_i mu_i=2. No PSD assumption, objective bound,
rank, or child oracle is used in the obstruction. Incoming child-terminal
coefficients in K may be present. In particular the ensuing argument is
stronger than one limited to already certified children.

Set

 theta=(3A+d)/(2S), s=1-theta=(A+d)/(2S).                 (6)

The right finite block is an EXACT copy and its optimum attachments have
the separate scale theta:

 lambda_(R_i,R_v)=K_iv, lambda_(R_i,star)=theta*mu_i.     (7)

This exact-copy condition and (6) are new candidate restrictions, not
conclusions drawn from the floating-point fits.

The base rule is kappa=0. Its ONLY repair is

                         kappa=s/(2d).                  (8)

For either choice, define the complete backward transport by

 lambda_(R_i,L_0)=mu_i*[s-kappa*d*(A-p_i)/A],
 lambda_(R_i,L_k)=mu_i*kappa*t*(A-p_i)/A,
 lambda_(R_i,L_l)=0 for 0<l<k.                           (9)

Retain the forward direction, with arbitrary nonnegative multipliers
lambda_(L_l,R_i) whose column totals are

 F_i=sum_l lambda_(L_l,R_i)=kappa*mu_i*(A-p_i).           (10)

Both directions are therefore explicitly present in the repaired rule.
If mu_i>0 and i<k, F_i>0; the repaired backward coefficient to L_0 is
strictly positive too. At the original terminal, p_k=A, so F_k=0,
lambda_(R_k,L_k)=0 and lambda_(R_k,L_0)=s*mu_k.

ALL left finite rows lambda_(L_l,L_v), and ALL left optimum attachments
nu_(L_l), may be chosen independently of any scalar child-copy law,
with arbitrary dependence on position, m and q. The obstruction grants
every nonnegative such choice for which full original flow holds. This
includes adding nonzero incoming edges at the bridge query L_k. There
is no assumption that it remains an unqueried child terminal.

Here is an explicit full nonnegative flow completion, showing that these
prescribed rows are not merely a hypothetical feasible-set description.
Put

 E=sum_i mu_i*(A-p_i), 0<=E<=2A.

Send every forward F_i from L_k; set all left finite pairs to zero; set

 nu_(L_0)=2s-kappa*d*E/A, nu_(L_k)=kappa*d*E/A,
 nu_(L_l)=0 for 0<l<k.                                  (11)

Every optimum-outgoing coefficient is zero. Together (7),(9)-(11) specify
every original multiplier. The theorem below also covers all the more
general left corrections and forward distributions just described.

### Exact signs and value cancellation

The inequalities 0<theta<1, s>0, d>0 and 0<=p_i<=A imply all the claimed
signs. In the repaired rule,

 s-kappa*d*(A-p_i)/A=s*(A+p_i)/(2A)>=s/2.

In (11), 0<=kappa*d*E/A<=s, so nu_(L_0)>=s>0 and nu_(L_k)>=0.
The base has nu_(L_0)=2s, nu_(L_k)=0. All other coefficients are
nonnegative multiples of mu_i or entries of K, or are explicitly zero.

The backward outgoing total at R_i is

 B_i=mu_i*[s+kappa*(A-p_i)]=s*mu_i+F_i.                 (12)

By (5), the right value flow is
(2*1_(i=k)-mu_i)+theta*mu_i+B_i-F_i=2*1_(i=k).
At L_0, the incoming backward sum equals the first quantity in (11).
At L_k, incoming backward minus outgoing forward is
kappa*(t/A-1)*E=kappa*d*E/A, equal to its optimum attachment.
All other left flows vanish in the explicit completion. Thus every
finite f_i cancels in (2), including f_n; the total optimum incoming
mass is 2. No interpolation row or value variable is silently omitted.

### Why these coefficients were tested

The base routes the right terminal's backward correction to the actual
initial left sample. If an input child has exact Huber contact and mu_k>0,
its terminal row equation is sum_v K_kv*(A-p_v)=A*mu_k.
For the parent, that row at exact Huber contact would require
S*theta*mu_k=A*mu_k+S*s*mu_k. Solving gives (6). Thus theta is derived
from this chosen terminal attachment in the original identity. It is
not a fitted numerical constant.

The base's right-node flow permits no forward mass. The sole repair
allows such mass to increase linearly with the remaining right mass
A-p_i and distributes backward mass between the original endpoints
L_0,L_k. Formula (9) has the required mass (12) and first moment
kappa*t*(A-p_i), which interpolates the two endpoint contact conditions.
Its interior contact defect is calculated explicitly in Section 9.
The largest kappa allowed by the displayed L_0 sign is s/d; (8) uses
half of this exact cap to leave that multiplier strictly positive.
This is one analytic repair, not a scan over coefficient choices.

The initial-point terminal destination is a substantive restriction of
the tested law. It need not match the numerical optimizers. In particular
this draft does not claim that all asymmetric recursions must use (6).

## 4. Feasible starting arrays and the recursion's actual scope

For H_0, the only nonzero multiplier lambda_(0,star)=2 at q=1 gives
M=||x_0-g_0||^2. For the actual H_1=(b), b=1+a, use

 lambda_(0,star)=lambda_(1,star)=lambda_(1,0)=1,

with every other original coefficient zero. Direct expansion gives

 M(1/(1+2b))
   =1/(1+2b)*||x_0-(1+2b)(g_0+g_1)/2||^2
       +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4.                         (13)

For the physical family, a<1/lambda_s=sqrt(2)-1, so
1<b<sqrt(2)<3/2; (13) is PSD on all three formal coordinates.
This base is part of the joint derivation, not a separately promoted fact.

Equations (5),(7),(9)-(11) define an actual nonnegative value-flow
recursion from this base for every later child, always with the enclosing
m fixed. They do NOT define a PSD recursion: that is precisely what the
full-space test below refutes. No new stronger child invariant is presumed,
and no virtual child oracle or primitive-capacity result replaces a parent
calculation. The full proof of failure even grants arbitrary arrays (5)
and arbitrary sign-valid left corrections at the final join.

## 5. The first full-space PSD obstruction

Evaluate the entire original quadratic on the following original-coordinate
linear subspace, in one real dimension:

 g_(L_l)=0 for every 0<=l<=k,
 g_(R_i)=v for every 0<=i<=k,
 x_0=x.                                                   (14)

The terminal gradient remains present and equals v in this TEST subspace;
no equality constraint has been imposed on the certificate. A PSD matrix
must remain nonnegative on every such subspace. The original substitutions
give x_(L_l)=x and x_(R_i)=x-p_i*v, including the terminal.
These formal test vectors are not asserted to satisfy interpolation or to
be an actual convex-function trajectory; they test the full dual matrix.

Every left finite quadratic and left optimum quadratic vanishes on (14),
irrespective of how large or position-dependent its coefficient is. The
right finite block contributes

 sum_(i,l)K_il*(p_i-p_l)*v^2
       =[2A-sum_i mu_i*p_i]*v^2,                         (15)

where (15) is just (5) multiplied by p_i and summed, not a use of
child PSD or an independent-gradient assumption about actual iterates.
The right optimum rows contribute
-2theta*x*v+theta*sum_i mu_i*(p_i+1/2)*v^2.
The backward rows contribute sum_i B_i*(p_i+1/2)*v^2;
the forward rows contribute sum_i F_i*v^2/2. Inserting (12) proves
the exact whole-parent identity

 M(q)|_(14)=q*x^2-2theta*x*v+Gamma*v^2,
 Gamma=1+2A+kappa*sum_i mu_i*(A-p_i)*(1+p_i).             (16)

In particular (16) is linked explicitly to ALL original rows and to the
original (n+2)-coordinate matrix (3)-(4). It is not an autonomous bridge
inequality, a relaxed child's bound, or a virtual matrix of lower order.

For q>0, set v=1 and x=theta/q. This is an explicit full-space direction,
whose quadratic value is

                         Gamma-theta^2/q.              (17)

If q<0, x alone already gives a negative direction. If q=0, the nonzero
coefficient -2theta*x*v in (16) does. Hence any PSD level in this law
is positive and necessarily satisfies q>=theta^2/Gamma.

## 6. A loss larger than 11/10, including the forward repair

For 0<=p<=A, completing a scalar square gives

 (A-p)(1+p)=(A+1)^2/4-[p-(A-1)/2]^2<=(A+1)^2/4.

Since sum mu_i=2, the repaired Gamma satisfies

 Gamma<=1+2A+s*(A+1)^2/(4d)=:Gamma_up.                   (18)

The unrepaired Gamma=1+2A is no larger, so it is enough to treat (18).
Suppose

                    A>=1024, 2/5<=r=d/A<=1/2.          (19)

Then theta=(3+r)/(2(2+r)), s=(1+r)/(2(2+r))<=3/10 and

 Gamma_up=A*[2+s/(4r)]+1+s/(2r)+s/(4rA).

The coefficient of A in 10D*theta^2-11Gamma_up is

 P(r)/[8r(2+r)],
 P(r)=40r^3+64r^2-3r-11.

For r=2/5+w with w>=0, direct symbolic expansion is

 P(r)=3/5+(337/5)w+112w^2+40w^3>=3/5.

Also 8r(2+r)<=10. Therefore

 10D*theta^2-11Gamma_up
   >=3A/50+10theta^2-11[1+s/(2r)+s/(4rA)]>0.           (20)

For the last strict inequality, s/(2r)<=3/8,
s/(4rA)<=3/(16*1024), so the bracket is less than 3/2;
3A/50>=3*1024/50>33/2. Every inequality is exact.

It follows that D*theta^2>(11/10)*Gamma. Consequently for every
chi=qD<=11/10 with q>0, (17) is strictly negative. This proves (0)
under (19). It applies even if all unspecified left multipliers and the
forward distribution are allowed to depend arbitrarily on q and m.
Such choices cannot change (16). If their signs or value flow fail,
they are already disqualified before this PSD test.

This is a restriction on certificate LEVELS IN THIS LAW. It is neither
a lower bound on unrestricted W nor an assertion that the law becomes
PSD at any larger level.

## 7. The unchanged physical hierarchy satisfies (19)

For the actual final join let j=m-1. Counting the occurrences of each
physical step, or proving the same identity by symbolic induction, gives

 A=2^j-1+a*(c^j-2^j)/(c-2), d=1+a*c^j.                 (21)

For m>=1536, put u=1/(4m). Since sqrt(2)<10/7, u<=1/256 gives
c<(17/7)(257/256)=4369/1792<49/20<5/2. Since sqrt(2)>7/5,
c>12/5. Also beta_word>=3/4, hence a>3/10.

Write z=(2/c)^j. Because c>12/5, z<(5/6)^j. The inequality
(5/6)^4=625/1296<1/2 and j>=24 give z<1/64.
Using (21), d>a*c^j, and 1/(c-2)<1/(lambda_s-2)=lambda_s,

 A/d < 1/(c-2)+z/a
       <17/7+5/96<5/2.                                (22)

Conversely, dropping the positive term 2^j-1 from (21) gives

 A>(d-1)*(1-z)/(c-2)
      >(20/9)*(63/64)*(d-1)=(35/16)*(d-1)>2d.          (23)

Here d>12 follows from a>3/10, c>2 and j>=6. Equations (22)-(23)
give 2/5<d/A<1/2. Finally every physical child step exceeds one, so
A>2^j-1>=2^11-1>1024. This proves (19) for EVERY m>=1536 without
replacing a child by a newly parameterized hierarchy or executing a
recurrence. Therefore (0) holds at the required true horizons.

## 8. Comparison with the ORIGINAL benchmark

The accepted unconditional ray theorem in fact042 states

 Z_1=lim_m lambda_s^(-m)*B_(2^m-1),
 Z_1>=lambda_s^(-j)*B_(2^j-1) for every j>=0.

Section 8 of that fact certifies every interval in the public fixed table
research/audit_c78/input_interval_table.csv, whose SHA256 is
2a7585bc0bade9f85461456c13d60bbe618f85c7cc816e1ddc5b4c1a1d611e68.
The row 1023 was read in this round, not recomputed. With

 D80=1208925819614629174706176,
 L1023=19205851554343006584447327614,

the accepted interval gives D80*B_1023>=L1023>15850*D80.
For an explicit integer comparison, the last difference is
44377313451134165354438014>0. Also
lambda_s^10=3363+2378sqrt(2)<6727, since sqrt(2)<99/70.
Thus the displayed accepted enclosure and elementary comparisons give

 Z_1>15850/6727>47/20,                                 (24)

where 20*15850-47*6727=831>0. No new DP, interval generation, numerical
root, arithmetic replay, or certificate checker was run.

For completeness, the exponential series and k!>=2*3^(k-2), k>=2,
give

 exp(1/4)<1+1/4+(1/32)/(1-1/12)=113/88<9/7.            (25)

There is strictness because the factorial inequality is strict from k=4.
Accepted fact5fd gives, for the unchanged tau=1/4 physical words,

 B_(n_m)/(1+2 sum H_m) -> Z_1/[2exp(1/4)]>329/360.     (26)

If there were a sequence of full PSD levels q_cert,m in the tested
family, (0) and (26) would imply

 liminf_m B_(n_m)*q_cert,m
    >=(11/10)*Z_1/[2exp(1/4)]>3619/3600>1.

The same statement holds along any unbounded subsequence of such levels.
This proves (1) and rules out a NEGATIVE-alternative certificate through
this law. It does not lower-bound W_(n_m)(H_m): a different multiplier
family could certify a smaller value.

## 9. Why an affine transport also loses exact Huber contact

This additional identity diagnoses the repair, without being a premise
of Sections 5-8. Suppose the reference child really has Huber contact
and mu_k>0. A Huber-tight full parent must also have contact. Indeed
take Hub(x)=x^2/2 for |x|<=1 and Hub(x)=|x|-1/2 otherwise, and its
actual trajectory from x_0=D. Every x_i=D-P_i>=S+1>1, so all finite
g_i=1 and f_i=x_i-1/2. Its terminal numerator 2f_n is D and its initial
square is D^2;
every finite Q_ij and every Q_(i,star) is zero, and
Q_(star,i)=1-x_i<0. Nonnegative multipliers and PSD at q=1/D force
optimum-outgoing multipliers to vanish and M(D,1,...,1)=0. A PSD
quadratic with zero value at a vector annihilates that vector: apply
it to that vector plus an arbitrary scalar multiple of another vector.

Writing P_i for original chronological mass, expansion of (3) gives
the finite-coordinate contact equations

 (2S-P_i)*nu_i=2h_i+sum_(v finite)lambda_iv*(P_i-P_v).  (27)

More explicitly, if e is the all-ones gradient vector, differentiating
(3) at equal gradients gives
2(Ce)_i=2h_i+(1+P_i)*nu_i+sum_v lambda_iv*(P_i-P_v).
In the position terms, full finite value flow makes the sum of the
position-derivative contributions 2h_i; the remaining derivative based
at i is its displayed outgoing drift. The norm terms contribute nu_i.
The gradient part of M(D,e)=0 is 2Ce=D*nu, which yields (27).

For the right finite copy (7), the child version of (27) subtracts out.
Let backward coefficients more generally have the form
lambda_(R_i,L_l)=mu_i*(b_l+c_l*p_i), with every actual coefficient
nonnegative. Permit arbitrary nonnegative forward coefficients, and
require their terminal column to be zero. Put
B0=sum b_l, B1=sum c_l, M0=sum p_l*b_l, M1=sum p_l*c_l.
Right flow gives F_i/mu_i=B0+B1*p_i-s at every mu_i>0.
The right equations (27) require

 (t+p_i)*(B0+B1*p_i)-(M0+M1*p_i)
       =theta*t-s*(2A-p_i).                            (28)

If there are at least three distinct positions with mu_i>0, equality
of the two degree-at-most-two polynomials at those positions forces
B1=0. At the terminal, zero forward inflow and mu_k>0 then give
B0=s. Thus every F_i=0. At mu_i=0, flow and the backward formula
also force F_i=0. Nonnegativity makes EVERY forward coefficient zero.

Consequently an affine backward source profile cannot retain genuine
forward mass while simultaneously preserving this exact finite copy,
a single right attachment scale, and Huber contact. This is scoped to
the stated law; it does not follow from the approximate numerical fits.

For the explicit repair (9), the backward first moment is
kappa*t*(A-p_i). At the endpoints p_i=0,A the intended right equation
is matched. At an interior position its drift exceeds the required drift
by exactly kappa*mu_i*p_i*(A-p_i). This provides the concrete interior
contact defect. The result of Sections 5-8 is stronger for the selected
theta,kappa: raising q within a small whole-word budget cannot repair
the full PSD failure, even if the contact requirement is abandoned.

# Independent C78 audit

The frozen candidate has SHA256 dbc2113a4d1064a0a81fc1a71d1eefc7136d906ecc3eb263a79d25b3f687f29e. This report records an independent audit, not a verifier verdict. No critical gap remains identified at the point of submission. The actual four-step seed is an explicit hypothesis and is absent.

1. Source mapping. A fresh download of arXiv2609.11788v1 matches the title and four authors. Proposition2.5 defines exactly the primitive all-split Bellman recursion with leaf count N=n+1 and U_1=1. Corollary4.2 explicitly extends U_0=0 and proves monotonicity and balanced optimality. Its proof via Theorems3.1 and4.1 was read, as was AppendixC.2. The a=0 identity is Omega(0,1)=1. The source's rho is the candidate's lambda; its exponent p is the candidate's rho. No asymmetric balanced rule, equivalence of ambient schedule classes, or qualitative phase theorem is used. Matlas returned unrelated results, so the external reference is grounded in the actual primary paper. The PDF extractor failed with `pdftotext: command not found`; primary arXiv HTML was downloaded and extracted with Python's standard library, without installation.

2. Growth and increments. The derivative of log((1+exp(z))^rho-1) gives the displayed positive second derivative, hence the power envelope. Both adjacent-ratio maps have derivative at most1/2 on[1,lambda] and preserve that interval. For the dominant adjacent bound, prefix m=1 uses the formal equality G(0,V_N)=V_N; m>=2 gives a legal original split after shortening the prefix. The estimate of the square-root increment uses V_N>=U_N before division. No square-root concavity is assumed.

3. Optimal spine. Direct expansion gives c^2-(a+b)c-ab=ab(t-u-Q)/u. When a<=d, Q(u+Q)>2a forces t<u+Q, so merging two primitive prefixes strictly improves the tree. Therefore every optimal two-edge continuation has U_m1>V_R. The power bounds then imply R<2m1 and R<(2/3)(m1+m2+R). This constrains actual maximizing trees rather than pruning allowed splits.

4. Every parity and boundary. Even-parent odd-prefix uses one legal coarse split. Even-parent even-prefix averages(a-1,b+1) and(a,b); a=0 is the formal equality(-1,n). Odd-parent odd-prefix averages parents n,n+1 and yields(Delta_n^2-Delta_n Delta_b)/4. Odd-parent even-prefix averages(a-1,b) and(a,b), including a=0, and yields Delta_n^2/4. In both odd cases the continuation argument is at most the midpoint parent value, so the quadratic derivative is at least that positive midpoint. Homogeneity and the 1-Lipschitz square-root continuation pass the error to the actual right child. The one-step recurrence is used only above the finite base, so delta_0 is never required.

5. Infinite tail and ray orientation. Every visited fine index is at least4096, so floor(t/2)>=2048. The two-edge leaf shrink gives a factor>4/3 between every other such coarse index. Summing each parity class backward yields at most64*2048^(-4/3)/(1-(3/4)^(4/3))<1/100. Doubling in the unshifted leaf count gives increasing lower rays; doubling the shifted index with the defect gives the upper ray. The upper index3840 has depth8 and the lower index3071 has depth10, leaving exactly lambda^2. Adjacent bounds cover the actual output offset4; it cannot be silently dropped at finite depth.

6. Finite certificate. The independent checker proves every upper interval against ALL original splits by two nonnegative polynomial comparisons, and each lower interval by one admissible split. Monotonicity and induction make that certificate sufficient without assuming any argmax exact. It then checks every square-root rounding and all4096 fine-index inequalities, including both endpoints. It passed16,773,120 upper-split inequalities and8,190 lower witnesses in5.589835043007042 seconds, exit0. All16 public manifest files matched. The old phase-DP ledger hash stayed f2da2787f2ef0bd340d7e46f1bffd7a88c7e35521659ddcca613d8cd45a6f831. The ONE new replay allowance is consumed. Exact endpoint orientation gives Theta<19299/2500 and B_3071>64000.

7. Primitive full-space matrix. The value coefficients of sum Lambda Q are L_flow^T e; the value coefficients of V_h are their negative. The position terms are sym(L_flow(T-I)), and the gradient-difference terms are sym(L_flow)-diag(omega)/2. Thus their sum with V_h is exactly D_prim+sym(L_flow T). No compression or formal gradient identification occurs. If optimum edges were included, the zero x_0 diagonal forces all outgoing finite-to-optimum coefficients to vanish; total value flow and nonnegativity then remove incoming optimum edges. Only certificate sufficiency is needed, so no unattained dual statement is hidden.

8. Primitive--primitive composition. Expanding the four groups in E_PP cancels all off-diagonal gradient terms and gives the displayed value and diagonal terms. The equation d(A+C+d)=AC proves all three coefficient identities. It also gives0<d<C and kappa>1. Thus every added interpolation coefficient is nonnegative, and any child PSD remainder is embedded in the entire parent formal space with coefficients1,kappa. Empty children and zero-valued seed steps cause no loss of coordinates.

9. Primitive--dominant composition. The mass identity is D*zeta^2=2A+zeta D=2sum(parent)+1. Expanding the retained final square and the right child's actual starting point produces exactly -2mu sum v_i Q_(i,p), including the right terminal sample and the empty-right-child case. All weights and added multipliers are nonnegative. The objective certificate is obtained by adding these interpolation terms to||x_0||^2-2D f_terminal; its PSD remainder is the retained final square plus the embedded child remainders. This holds for every smooth convex function.

10. Actual word and normalization. A full n-node dominant tree has exactly n primitive leaves and one dominant leaf. Replacing just the primitive leaves by four physical steps gives precisely5n output steps. Scaling the untouched dominant leaf from1 toA is an auxiliary scalar comparison, not a physical extra block. Its square-root error is at most sqrt(A)-1 along the entire spine. The full certificate yields the upper bound1/Dsub_n and the Huber instance from accepted0be8b54e0a33381c yields equality with true initial distance1, for all nonnegative steps. Multiplying the objective by L preserves W=2R.

11. Fixed horizon. For j>=25, B_(3*2^j-1)>64000*lambda^15>10^10, so the untouched-leaf relative square-root loss is less than10^-5. The strict scalar bound gives Theta/A<1-1/19300<1-2/50000, while the squared retained factor is greater than1-1/50000. Their quotient is strictly less than1-1/50000. This includes A=193/25. No claim of an actual seed or of original minimax resolution is used.

The selected submission rewrites historical status text, uses the project symbol C_n for primitive capacity, and uses rootB_n and Hsub_n/Dsub_n to avoid conflicting glossary meanings. It includes a standalone finite defining algorithm and the sufficient independent checker. These are notation and audit additions, with no alteration of the claimed conditional mathematics.
